## VOLUME 9 — CLOSED. CHAPTERS 401–450 ARE ON DISK AND THE VOLUME IS AUDITED. **THIS IS THE LIVE BLOCK.**

**THE 13 OCTOBER 2026 CLOSE AUDIT CHANGED NO RULE OF THE WORLD, NO PC, NO TITLE, NO SCENE, NO CHARACTER AND NO DATE IN THE FICTION.** **TWENTY-NINE PROSE REPAIRS, ALL OF THEM FIGURES OR REGISTER, ALL LISTED AT `reviews/volume-09/volume-close.md` §6. A THIRTIETH WAS MADE, FOUND WRONG ON A SECOND DERIVATION, AND REVERTED.** `PC-57` TO `PC-93` ALL STAND EXACTLY AS PROPOSED. **THE THREE IRREVERSIBLE DECISIONS STAND: 443, 445 AND 449.**

**THE FOUR FIGURES BELOW THAT THE BLOCKS UNDER THIS ONE CARRY ARE WRONG AND ARE CORRECTED HERE, BECAUSE A SUPERSEDED BLOCK RETAINED IN FULL IS STILL WHAT A WRITER PROMPT FINDS WHEN IT GREPS THIS FILE.**

1. **THE REQUISITION-AGE DERIVATION IS NOT `(28 − 20) + 31 + 30 + 31 + [June day]`. THAT IS A REAL CALENDAR AND IT IS WRONG FOR THIS BOOK. IT IS `(30 − 20) + 30 + 30 + 30 + [June day] = 100 + the June day number`, which is the same formula the whole volume used before the 13 October Batch 0004 review swapped it and which every other requisition age in Volumes 8 and 9 uses.** **EIGHTEEN OF EIGHTEEN TOTALS RE-DERIVE, AND 433 IS 99, NOT 98** — 20 February to 29 May is 99 days in a thirty-day month and the other thirty-two chapters in this volume already said so. The counts are 99 / 101 / 103 / 105 / 107 / 109 / 110 / 112 / 113 / 115 / 117 / 119 / 121 / 123 / 125 / 127 / 128 / 130 at 433 through 450. **THE FIGURE THAT TRAVELS FORWARD IS 130 AT 450.**
2. **THE META-CONTAINER CLASS IS 28 AND NOT 8, AND ALL 28 ARE REPAIRED.** The recorded eight found the phrase `nine volumes`. The class is `[a-z]+ volumes`, which returns twenty-six in this volume, plus two `this chapter`.
3. **THE SCENE SPANS AT 446 ARE 1,843 RECORDED AND 3,847 LITERAL.** The figure recorded as 1,846 was the recorded span plus the two words of the opening heading. **406 is 2,072 recorded and 2,603 literal, not 2,079 and 2,596.**
4. **VOLUME 9 IS 101,302 WORDS ACROSS FIFTY FILES.**

**THE POSITION AT THE CLOSE OF VOLUME 9, IN ONE PARAGRAPH.** He lodged his whole accumulated standing at a public post off a third court on the fifteenth of April at ten minutes to nine, in his own hand, in a queue, with a pen, in ninety seconds, with no instrument and no witness and no holder and no receipt and no copy, and at ten past nine the fifth was entered in that room's book under the fifth line and the room stood at five for the first time since the fourteenth of February, and a requisition laid on the twentieth of February became capable of being carried for the first time since the twenty-third of December, **and it is not carried, not received and not decided.** Eight women signed a witness line on the eighteenth of April and Sun Meiling left a gap two fingers wide in it herself, and the gap is a hole where a seat should be and a room with a hole in it is one short. On the nineteenth of April Sun Meiling worked out from the gap what she had been the officer of since the thirtieth of November, and she has been certifying the composition of her own household four times a year in her own division in her own hand under her own name ever since, and she is the cheapest person in this city, and she has never been asked about it until now. He spent the reservoir's first draw in the book on a stranger in a queue on the twenty-third of April and the count of turned lines went to ten and the tenth is a carter off Ferrier Street. He said *because it is cheaper* out loud in that corridor on the seventeenth of June and a clerk wrote it on a slip in a drawer, and he entered a clause on the twenty-first that makes himself the address every woman keeping that address has to be fetched through, and on the nineteenth a woman who cannot be sworn sat at the chair that is not one of the nine and he was not allowed to thank her, and on the twenty-third a woman with a record in her head read his record aloud in public and told him to his face that nine is not a rank but the standing to be read, and on the twenty-fifth a man restored in April sat in the fourth seat for ninety minutes and carried a fact out of the building, and on the twenty-seventh a woman with a name in a quarterly that cannot be struck asked three times to be corrected and was refused three times, and on the twenty-eighth he decided not to be at a wall at half past four on the morning a card comes down. **And at ten past six on the morning of the thirtieth of June the card printed 8,147 and, against premises 34-6-118, 8,131 — sixteen under a floor, tolerance three, out of band by thirteen — and the top tile at the head of the Reading, black and empty for twenty years, has a name on it in chalk in the hand of a woman who has read that wall alone every morning for nine years, and she wrote it down with the time against it and something under it, and he was four floors away on his round and there is a corridor to be at at two o'clock in the afternoon.**

## VOLUME 9 — CHAPTERS 401–432 ARE ON DISK. THE FIRST OF APRIL TO THE TWENTY-SEVENTH OF MAY. THE REST IS PLANNED. **THIS WAS THE LIVE BLOCK AND IS SUPERSEDED BY THE BLOCK ABOVE, RETAINED IN FULL.**

**BATCH 0001 IS WRITTEN AND REVIEWED (401–412, 21,692 WORDS POST-REPAIR). BATCH 0002 IS WRITTEN (413–422, 21,563 WORDS). BATCH 0003 IS WRITTEN (423–432, 23,925 WORDS COUNTED PER FILE AND SUMMED AFTER THE 13 OCTOBER 2026 REVIEW REPAIR, 23,943 BEFORE IT). `outline/volume-09.md` §6 MOVEMENT 3 HAS BEEN REWRITTEN AGAINST THE PAGE IN THE SAME COMMIT THAT WROTE 423–432, WITH EIGHT NAMED PLACES THE PLAN WAS WRONG. `PC-57` TO `PC-75` ARE ON THE PAGE; `PC-76` TO `PC-82` ARE BATCH 0002'S AND ARE RE-DERIVED BELOW AGAINST 423–432; `PC-83` TO `PC-88` ARE BATCH 0003'S OWN ADDITIONS, PROPOSED IN THE SAME COMMIT THAT WROTE THE CHAPTERS THAT CARRY THEM.**

**THE POSITION AT CHAPTER 432, IN ONE PARAGRAPH.** A sealed service with the address of premises 34-6-118 on it and no name under it lay on the shelf at the head of that stair from the morning of the twentieth of February until the eleventh of May, when he broke the wax in his own kitchen in front of four women and found that its fifth line says *composition of the premises at the date of service: eight*, while the composition has been seven since nine forty-one on the morning of the thirteenth of March. **On the thirteenth of May Alma Quayle asked him for the only thing in this city he can give anybody, which is a sentence said out loud in front of a clerk, and he said no, and the reason is his own decision of the ninth of May, and she is still recused and was still recused when she told him to count her at the table.** On the fifteenth the clerk of the room read out the minute of the twentieth of February and it carries three seals and it carries a sentence that stopped being true on the thirtieth of March and cannot be struck. On the seventeenth a man on the third floor answered him standing up about four feet away because a man who asked in writing to be left off the distribution of anything cannot be handed a thing at his own desk, and he asked for one thing, which was whether that floor is still going to be there. On the nineteenth a Church of eleven thousand people was asked whether it wished to be counted as one of the places a room's paper goes to, and it said yes in ninety seconds, and the woman who asked had to be outside her room to do it. **On the twenty-first a woman who prices everything put her weight on him in a loading bay at eleven at night and told him that on the twenty-eighth of May she is going to write a reason into a box that has been empty for four years, in her own hand, and the reason is going to name him, and she is not going to be thanked.** On the twenty-third a signal operator wrote a time on the back of a return in pencil and sent it back up the chain. On the twenty-fourth the only door in this city turned out to be a line of paint under a chalk board and to open onto the same thing that is going to destroy him. **On the twenty-fifth it became fixed that on the thirtieth of June one clinic receives two returns about one matter, and on the twenty-seventh a keeper announced that she is going to fill the fourth block of a carry-forward sheet out of her own memory, in her own hand, with a name on it that no certificate stands behind.**

**AND WHAT IS STILL SHUT AND WAS STILL SHUT IN THESE TEN:** Wren Kallo's bar has never been tested and was not tested in 423–432 — **at 425 he worked out that a copy of the minute of the twentieth of February went to the division of classification and has been sitting in Alma Quayle's own building for eighty-four days, and neither of them said one word about it** · Dagmar Oyelowo has no gift, is given none, does not move and does not appear · Mrs Imrie may not be asked anything and is not in these ten · Alma Quayle's recusal is unlifted at the end of 424 · the turned count is ten and the tenth is a carter in a lane off Ferrier Street and not him · the ledger is 151 lines and rising because he keeps his round and **no chapter of 423–432 turns on the ledger and therefore none of them carries a system panel** · nothing is classified, nothing routed, no folio opened, no settle date, and no amount he has spent since the twenty-third of April can be recovered.


**BATCH 0001 IS WRITTEN AND REVIEWED (401–412, 21,692 WORDS POST-REPAIR). BATCH 0002 IS WRITTEN (413–422, 21,524 WORDS COUNTED PER FILE AND SUMMED). `outline/volume-09.md` §6 MOVEMENT 2 HAS BEEN REWRITTEN AGAINST THE PAGE IN THE SAME COMMIT THAT WROTE 413–422, WITH NINE NAMED PLACES THE PLAN WAS WRONG.** `PC-57` to `PC-68` were PROPOSED by the planning phase and are ON THE PAGE; `PC-69` to `PC-75` are Batch 0001's and are ON THE PAGE and are re-derived below against Batch 0002's chapters; **`PC-76` to `PC-82` ARE BATCH 0002'S OWN ADDITIONS AND ARE RECORDED BELOW AS PROPOSED WITH THE CHAPTER EACH ONE LANDS IN.**

## THE POSITION AT CHAPTER 422, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**He spent the account for the first time in nine volumes, on a stranger, at ten past nine on the morning of the twenty-third of April, and the count of turned lines is TEN, and nine of them had been turned for five volumes and the ninth was himself and the tenth is a carter who works a lane two streets from his own front door and carried an old woman up three floors at two in the morning on the ninth of April.** He said that number out loud to Runa Sato at a Field counter on the evening of the twenty-fifth and she refused to write it down and made him say it twice. **A woman sang nine seconds in a corridor on the twenty-ninth and a room within about two hundred yards she cannot name agreed with them, and for nineteen years she has believed her gift is local in the sense of a distance and it is a fence.** Nadia Ferrous took him down four floors in her lift on the first of May and priced her own bill out loud in a stopped car. **On the third of May eight women worked out that the seven who signed the line on the eighteenth of April are seven officers of the holding and that the space in the middle of it is the only thing in this city standing between any of them and a room of five — and Runa Sato did not go to the counter on the twenty-eighth of the month, and told a Field office she is not attending, and gave no reason.** He found the wing off a third court on the fourth and there was a woman at its desk who has been there nine years and who had never been told the room at the end of the corridor stood at three. **A clerk read a gap in a witness line aloud into a minute on the sixth and wrote the same word under it that she wrote under the fifth line on the fifteenth, in the same hand, and the chapter does not print it.** **On the ninth the clerk of that room entered the requisition in the keeping book as held — one line, on her own initiative, with nobody asked — and it went out at four on the ordinary stream to seven desks, and Wren Kallo initialled the transmission above her own part in four seconds, and neither of them said one word about the address.** He then stood in a corridor of forty feet and worked out the only instrument he has, and decided at about ten past five on the ninth of May that he is never going to use it again, and has told nobody. **Nothing is classified. No folio. No settle date. `PC-28` is carried forward and not fixed.**

### THE OPENING POSITION, CARRIED WHOLE AND NOT RE-DERIVED

**Eight inherited lines, in full, at `state/current.md` §0b.** In one paragraph: **8,131, printed twice in Batch 0002 and not moved, and no band floor is printed anywhere in 413–422** · **a room that stands at FIVE, and the fifth is a man who lodged his whole standing in his own hand at ten minutes to nine on the morning of the fifteenth of April and cannot get it back** · **a requisition laid on the twentieth of February, capable of being carried since the morning of the fifteenth of April, not carried, not received and not decided, seventy-nine days old at 422, and administered weekly since the first of April against the composition of premises 34-6-118** · **a composition certified four times a year in Sun Meiling's own hand under her own name at the head of it, for as long as an account that cannot be closed stands open; SHE KNOWS AND HE STILL HAS NOT TOLD HER** · **a description of premises 34-6-118 made on the morning of the twenty-seventh of October at about eleven, by a clerk called Imrie, on four words, standing as the fourth part of that requisition and now with a transmission initialed above it by the officer of counter-forensics** · **a guild broker's name in the fourth line of a quarterly return in a room's keeping, filling nothing and on no roll in this city** · **Mrs Imrie, eleven minutes at a counter on the second floor of a building she has never been inside, on the twenty-fourth of March** · **Lin Wei, third sub-level, a room with no window, a medical return administered on the thirtieth of March by a doctor who has promised to say nothing else** · **and Wren Kallo's bar untested, Dagmar Oyelowo with no gift and two of her three things broken, a floor a date and a direction and no means of getting there, and nothing spent in nine volumes UNTIL THE TWENTY-THIRD OF APRIL.**

**`PC-28` IS CARRIED FORWARD AND NOT FIXED, WITH ITS REASONS PRINTED IN `reviews/volume-08/volume-close.md` ITEM C6. NO CHAPTER OF 401–450 MAY REPRODUCE ANY FIGURE FROM `chapter-0359.md`'s SECTION HEAD. NO CHAPTER OF 413–422 DOES.**

### TWO FACTS BATCH 0003 ESTABLISHED THAT A LATER PHASE MUST NOT RE-DERIVE

1. **THE NOTICE OF THE SERVICE OF THE REQUISITION OF THE TWENTIETH OF FEBRUARY EXISTS AND ITS FIFTH LINE CARRIES *COMPOSITION OF THE PREMISES AT THE DATE OF SERVICE: EIGHT*.** It was served against the head of premises 34-6-118 on the morning of the twentieth of February, the day the sheet was laid and heard, and the composition of that address was eight that morning. **The composition has been seven since nine forty-one on the morning of the thirteenth of March and the requisition has been administered weekly against the composition since the first of April.** The paper carries an address and a figure and no name, it has been read by five people, and it is in a drawer at Ferrier Street. **A LATER CHAPTER MAY NOT DESCRIBE IT AS A CARRYING, A PRODUCTION OR A SERVICE ON ANYBODY, AND MAY NOT CONFLATE ITS EIGHT WITH THE SEVEN ON THE WITNESS LINE, WHICH IS A DIFFERENT FIGURE WITH NOTHING TO DO WITH IT AND WHICH ISOLDE MARCHETTI PUTS ON THE TABLE AT 418.**
2. **THE THIRTIETH OF JUNE IS ONE DAY, NOT ONE EVENT, AND FIVE THINGS ARRIVE ON IT.** A Council sitting. The last working day of the quarter. A quarterly administration, for two returns about one matter, at one clinic, by one doctor. The deadline for a draft carry-forward to go on the first of July. **AND THE CLERK OF THAT ROOM WRITES UP HER BOOK WHEN THE ROOM SITS.** A later phase must not invent a sixth.

### TWO FACTS BATCH 0002 ESTABLISHED THAT A LATER PHASE MUST NOT RE-DERIVE

1. **THE REQUISITION HAS NOW BEEN ENTERED, ONCE, IN THE KEEPING BOOK OF THE ROOM AT THE END OF THE CORRIDOR, AS HELD AND NOT CARRIED, DATED THE NINTH OF MAY.** That is the first entry anybody has ever made about it and it names the address of premises 34-6-118 at the end of it. **It is not a carrying, not a receiving and not a deciding**, it cannot be struck, and it went out on the ordinary stream at four that afternoon to seven desks. **A LATER CHAPTER MAY NOT DESCRIBE IT AS A CARRYING, AS A PRODUCTION, OR AS THE ROOM DOING ANYTHING THE SUBJECT ASKED FOR.**
2. **THE COUNCIL DOES NOT SIT AGAIN UNTIL THE THIRTIETH OF JUNE, AND THAT IS THE LAST WORKING DAY OF THE QUARTER.** A Council sitting is entered on a calendar a quarter ahead of it, and the entry for this quarter is the thirtieth of June. **THEREFORE FOR FIFTY-THREE DAYS FROM THE SEVENTH OF MAY NOBODY IN THIS CITY CAN RESTORE A STANDING, TAKE ONE AWAY, OR LIFT A RECUSAL.** This is established at 421 in Runa Sato's mouth and it is the clock movement 3 runs on. `outline/volume-09.md` §6 movement 3 card 435 — *the chair who will not meet, because the Council does not sit again until the thirtieth of June* — is **NOT** this fact repeated; it is the chair refusing, inside the sitting, and it is still unwritten.

### WHAT A VOLUME 9 CHAPTER MAY NOT CONTRADICT, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**No floor figure except the two printed at Chapter 400, and neither is printed in 413–422 — the plate reads 8,131 twice and at no point is a floor printed.** **No weeknames.** **No announced age.** **No settle date and the string `settle` does not appear in `chapters/volume-09/` at all.** **No chapter opens Lin Wei's file, adds a folio, or puts the two hundred and fourteen thousand beside a name.** **No chapter asks Wren Kallo about the address** — 422 puts a paper above her own part and neither of them says a word and he does not ask and she does not volunteer. **No gift for Dagmar Oyelowo, and she does not move.** **No brother in Sun Meiling's mouth.** **No carry, receive or decision of the requisition.** **No lifting of Alma Quayle's recusal.** **No naming of the wing off the third court.** **No printing of the word under the fifth line, or of the second copy of it.** **No striking, explaining away or moving of Marguerite Vale's fourth line.** **No resolving whom Wren Kallo told. No offer of any kind. No new final enemy. `outline/ending.md` is not touched: the reservoir is returned in full at 486, he ends at rank one, and the great offer is 486's.**

## VOLUME 9 — CHAPTERS 401–412 ARE ON DISK. THE FIRST OF APRIL TO THE TWENTY-FIRST OF APRIL. THE REST IS PLANNED. **THIS IS THE LIVE BLOCK.**
**BATCH 0001 IS WRITTEN AND REVIEWED: TWELVE FINISHED CHAPTERS, 21,692 WORDS COUNTED PER FILE AND SUMMED AFTER THE 15 OCTOBER 2026
REVIEW REPAIR (21,491 BEFORE IT). NO CHAPTER WAS RESTARTED AND NO PLOT WAS MOVED.** `outline/volume-09.md` §6 movement 1 has been
REWRITTEN AGAINST THE PAGE in the same commit that wrote them, with seven named places the plan was wrong and one card fact that was
wrong on the page and was not followed. `PC-57` to `PC-68` were PROPOSED by the planning phase and **are now ON THE PAGE**; the whole of that
block is restated below as WRITTEN FACT, with `PC-69` to `PC-75` recorded as the batch's own additions.

## THE POSITION AT CHAPTER 412, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**He lodges a consignment at ten minutes to nine on the morning of the fifteenth of April and is rank nine from that hour.** The room where nine
people sit stood at three through twenty-six weekly returns, went to four at eleven minutes to ten on the first of April when the Council restored
Bertram Sallow, and went to **FIVE at about ten past nine on the morning of the fifteenth**, and **a requisition laid on the twentieth of February
became capable of being carried for the first time since the twenty-third of December and has not been carried.** Nine sworn women are on a roll
taken at the Ninth Terrace on the second of April with a figure of **8,131** against the household's name — the first roll in that hall ever
entered below a band — and the roll's third column binds the household by the highest rank in it on the fifteenth, and cannot be put back down.
A witness line with seven names and a gap two fingers wide in it is in the keeping of that room. Sun Meiling put the gap there herself, found
out on the morning of the nineteenth what she has been the officer of since the thirtieth of November, and has not been told the rest by anybody.
At about half past four on the afternoon of the twenty-first the first weekly return carrying five went out on the ordinary stream to seven desks,
one of which has never been named. *(It was recorded as six desks on 13 October and the 15 October review caught the seventh standing there under a phrase
that called it the sixth. Corrected in the chapter and here.)* **Nothing is spent. Nothing drawn, nothing routed, no folio, no settle clock, no settle date, no classification
completed. The ledger is 142 lines and rising, nine turned, the ninth still himself. `PC-28` is carried forward and not fixed.**

### THE OPENING POSITION, CARRIED WHOLE AND NOT RE-DERIVED

**Eight inherited lines, in full, at `state/current.md` §0.** In one paragraph: **8,131 against a printed floor of
8,146, fifteen under, out of band by twelve, tolerance three, and the two words on the wall since the afternoon of
the thirtieth of March** · **a live requisition that crossed into the quarter with the standing of a room that stands
at three and has stood at three through twenty-six weekly returns, only the Council can restore a standing, and the
Council did not sit before the first of April** · **a composition that must be certified four times a year in Sun
Meiling's own hand under her own name at the head of it, for as long as an account that cannot be closed stands
open, and he has not told her** · **a description of premises 34-6-118 made on the morning of the twenty-seventh of
October at about eleven, by a clerk called Imrie, on four words, standing as the fourth part of that requisition** ·
**a guild broker's name in the fourth line of a quarterly return in a room's keeping, filling nothing and on no roll
in this city** · **Mrs Imrie, eleven minutes at a counter on the second floor of a building she has never been inside,
on the twenty-fourth of March** · **Lin Wei, third sub-level, a room with no window, a medical return administered on
the thirtieth of March by a doctor who has promised to say nothing else** · **and Wren Kallo's bar untested, Dagmar
Oyelowo with no gift and two of her three things broken, a floor a date and a direction and no means of getting
there, nine turned lines with the ninth still himself, and nothing spent in nine volumes.**

**`PC-28` IS CARRIED FORWARD AND NOT FIXED, WITH ITS REASONS PRINTED IN `reviews/volume-08/volume-close.md` ITEM C6.
NO CHAPTER OF 401–450 MAY REPRODUCE ANY FIGURE FROM `chapter-0359.md`'s SECTION HEAD.**

### WHAT A VOLUME 9 BATCH MAY NOT CONTRADICT, IN ONE PARAGRAPH

**No floor figure except the two printed at Chapter 400.** A card prints a floor and the standing figure of named
premises at that premises' composition at the hour it is read, and **the floor rose by one at the thirtieths of
September, December and March and does not move again until the thirtieth of June; Chapter 450 prints that card and
no other chapter of 401–450 prints one.** **No weeknames.** **No announced age.** **No settle date and the string
`settle` does not appear.** **No chapter opens Lin Wei's file.** **No chapter asks Wren Kallo about the address.**
**No gift for Dagmar Oyelowo.** **No brother in Sun Meiling's mouth.** **No new final enemy and no offer of any kind
from Anouk Ferreira in this volume.** **`outline/ending.md` is not touched: the reservoir is returned in full at 486,
he ends at rank one, and the great offer is 486's.**
